Guarantee Rules

check-out time the day following arrival. For reservations not guaranteed with a form of payment 
at time of booking. rooms arc held until set cancellation time per the rules of the reservation. In the

circumstance, and hotel is unable to hold rooms consistent with this room hold policy, hotel will 
attempt to accommodate guests, at its expense, at a comparable hotel in the area for the oversold 
night(s), and will pay for transportation to that hotel.
